## 8.0.0-rc.416 — 2026-10-23

Open the shared screen immediately while private startup data is pending. Hydrate only its current lifecycle, retaining the existing Sheet, history, scroll and native account/cart owners. Closing, switching screens and a newer journey cancel stale completion. Failed private reads expose a bounded explicit retry rather than guessing guest identity. Dock and attributed launchers share this owner. Retain RC415’s head-printer repair, intentional transition reading and bounded native cart work. Signed RC416 is accepted on lab, National and Dharwad with native/browser evidence and exact retirement; physical cross-document reading and separate native page/cache/media work retain their explicit limits. [Acceptance](docs/APP115-RESPONSIVENESS-RC416-2026-10-23.md).

## 8.0.0-rc.415 — 2026-10-23

Use the canonical DSA_DIR when printing the early transition reader. RC414 remains a lab-only failed candidate: syntax and source gates missed the undefined alias in the rendered head. A new actual-printer regression and native frontend boot check cover it. Keep RC414’s scoped reading and bounded cart-work changes for fresh native/browser qualification.

## 8.0.0-rc.414 — 2026-10-23

Use readable active-colour transition facts and preserve an explicit hover, touch hold or keyboard read across a native document change while the next page loads immediately. Normal navigation retains zero dwell; release and cancellation cannot replay a reader on a later visit. Home presentation remains unchanged.

Remove two full Cart reads from ordinary pageshow, retain one fresh private hydration for back/forward cache restores, and reuse a completed native Woo mutation calculation in its full response. Read-only startup admin-Ajax defers the technical editor while retaining original cookie, origin and no-store boundaries. Stock, pricing, fees, discounts, shipping, fragments and other administration remain native. Browser and native performance evidence remain distinct.

## 8.0.0-rc.413 — 2026-10-23

Android install actions call an available native browser prompt directly in the icon activation stack. Remove the extra Kiwe OK screen; keep honest browser guidance when direct installation is unavailable. Shared Home/footer install groups explain Home Screen installation and optional notifications inline. iOS help no longer selects external-notification preferences, respects the explicitly chosen platform and describes current Share/Add to Home Screen steps.

Start native page requests immediately and release transition screens synchronously when content is ready. Retire legacy dwell timers, pointer-hover holds and competing post-ready view animations. Message selection avoids consecutive repeats across documents. Home setup clearly distinguishes website entry from later inactivity. No new navigation engine, poller or permission master is added.

Defer the technical Admin class on public requests, preserving full admin/admin-Ajax registration and the original authorized frontend-toolbar callback. This avoids parsing the approximately 700 KB class on ordinary public requests, including hosts whose OPcache file-size ceiling excludes it. Performance and compatibility acceptance remain separate from source-size measurements.

## 8.0.0-rc.412 — 2026-10-23

Let Home return after each uninterrupted inactivity interval rather than consuming its browsing-session eligibility. Keep only the optional immediate entry welcome session-limited. Suspend the timer while a sheet is open, restart a full interval on dismissal, and invalidate a pending lazy appearance when browsing resumes. Keep existing typing, hidden-tab and protected-task guards and the ten-second default.

Restore Home's original differentiated supporting-text and accent-clock colours by removing the later uniform dark-text override. Retain transparent glass, the upward cue, keyboard/swipe dismissal and existing install actions. App setup now describes recurring inactivity and exposes its interval for either entry choice. No business-data, setting, schema or permission migration is introduced.

Retain RC410’s normalization of WordPress string account references and all RC408 App/Users/Navigation corrections.

# Changelog

## 8.0.0-rc.378 — F4 source-runtime dependency admission, 2026-10-17 (lab and approved Dharwad accepted)

- Founder-approved [Dharwad rollout](docs/DHARWAD-FRAMEWORK-RC378-ROLLOUT-2026-10-17.md) installs the immutable accepted runtime and reviewed 17-document native delta. Fresh preflight, exact code/native recovery, native apply/readback, actual served/browser acceptance and independent cleanup pass. No product/transaction/provider data is restored. Zero framework development or rollout batches remain; National Chikki discovery is next.

- Generated templates and package manifests distinguish embedded and Kiwe-cached source JavaScript. Cached delivery requires the exact target capability and an explicit dependency; malformed, unknown or contradictory declarations fail packaging/native validation. Native layout regeneration preserves delivery. Existing portable exports and Bricks code permissions retain their original authority. No new public request, polling or tracking owner is added. [Combined native/browser/publication acceptance](docs/SEAM-F4-RUNTIME-CONTRACTS-2026-10-17.md): signed lab RC378, 163 composed Kiwe gates, 339 compiler tests/both builds, 633+7+55 native checks and exact state preservation. Published source 0f7679c passes both-host corpus, cached-target and accessibility parity. Original failed attempts remain retained; helpers/jobs are retired and ten inputs protected. Zero framework development batches remain. [The approved Dharwad rollout](docs/DHARWAD-FRAMEWORK-RC378-ROLLOUT-2026-10-17.md) is accepted on RC378 with its reviewed native delta and exact prior-state recovery retained.

## 8.0.0-rc.377 — F3 saved CSS selector boundary, 2026-10-17 (lab accepted)

- Apply the same narrow selector lexer to rule preludes in saved CSS controls. Declarations, URLs, quoted data, comments, custom-property blocks and at-rule conditions remain unchanged. Native restricted Bricks saves and raw/Framework materialization use this boundary; no page-specific CSS patch is introduced. The signed RC376 candidate exposed the residual selector case during strict stylesheet parsing; its original receipts remain retained. [Final lab acceptance](docs/NATIVE-REUSE-F3-2026-10-17.md) passes native role/save, exact readback, actual stylesheet parsing, responsive/browser checks and independent cleanup. Production stays RC372.

## 8.0.0-rc.376 — F3 native combinator syntax, 2026-10-17 (superseded by RC377)

- Extend the same native selector lexer to encoded child combinators, including `&amp;&gt;div`; keep strings, attributes, comments and escapes intact. This completes the additional case found by strict parsing of the imported lab stylesheet. Shared source-runtime bytes and native style ownership remain unchanged from RC375.

## 8.0.0-rc.375 — F3 first native compiler candidate, 2026-10-17 (superseded by RC376)

- One generated, versioned compiler bridge serves explicitly dependent native templates; standalone conversion retains its guarded fallback. Target calibration advertises support before new conversions choose the cached bridge. Native Bricks HTML/code permission and sanitation remain authoritative.
- Repair encoded nesting anchors only in advanced selector syntax at the authorized builder-save boundary. Quoted values, escapes, comments and executable markup retain their original handling. The explicit project Framework pass can promote exact repeated typography to native editable global classes while leaving geometry, records, queries and residual CSS local. Lab acceptance is pending.

## 8.0.0-rc.374 — F2 public runtime and Search, 2026-10-17 (lab accepted)

- Generate one versioned public token/vocabulary catalog from the existing PHP owners. Keep sanitized site values in HTML and reconstruct the existing browser APIs before consumers run. Parse the complete public element registry only on inspection, and load health-check implementation through the existing requested module loader.
- Classic Search keeps its controls visible and scrolls results in one flexible body; modern Sheets retain their shared shell. Cart, checkout, authentication, notification and native page/template bodies keep their existing owners. The measured CSS split was rejected because its added requests outweighed the tiny initial saving. [Ownership and tradeoffs](docs/decisions/2026-10-16-public-runtime-metadata.md). [F2 acceptance](docs/LEAN-RUNTIME-F2-2026-10-17.md) records 161 composed source gates, 630 deployment/seven boot/24 native checks, fresh Chrome continuity, exact restoration and cleanup. Production remains RC372; combined review and approval belong to F4.

## 8.0.0-rc.373 — F1 private dashboard foundation, 2026-10-16 (lab accepted)

- Build the 17 private task apps through one shared CSS owner, retaining necessary declaration-order exceptions. Native document dependencies load the versioned foundation once; PHP Home receives only its token projection. Complete workspace tokens preserve the existing task/Home palettes. No public page or Dock dependency or new browser JavaScript is added.
- The [F1 report](docs/WORKSPACE-FOUNDATION-F1-2026-10-16.md) records 159 composed source gates, 627 deployment/seven boot/200 native style checks and thirteen desktop/phone views. Combined CSS artifacts shrink 41.7%; actual shared-file cache reuse is verified, while a cold Posts load increases by 5.3 KB. Recovery and complete cleanup are retained. Dharwad remains RC372 and requires review and approval of the new combined candidate.

- Rename and delete WordPress menu groups from Frontend navigation. Deletion requires a fresh link fingerprint and explicit confirmation, preserves linked content, and protects menus assigned to theme locations.
- A group's New link dialog now adds to that group only. The global New link entrance retains group choice.
- Split Home into Daily and Setup tabs. Woo-enabled, permitted Products and Orders now have separate direct tiles; Guests uses one label; Notifications stays behind the bell.
- Unify card-level Indexable and Show in menu control styling with the shared Metro control source.

## 8.0.0-rc.197 — consistent category-dialog exit

- Put a top Close control on the remaining native Categories creation dialog, matching Frontend navigation and the shared menu picker. Escape and Close cannot dismiss a save in progress.

## 8.0.0-rc.196 — title-only menu search and settled card picker

- Search published pages, posts, products and connected services by title in Frontend navigation, matching the field label; category search already uses names.
- Wait for a fresh menu read before displaying groups in a card picker, so an older hidden/visible state does not flash while the dialog opens.
- Disable group creation and activation controls at the same limits enforced by the native writer.

## 8.0.0-rc.195 — client Frontend navigation acceptance repair

- Admit the exact private menu-group route through the client Administrator's outer REST boundary while keeping unrelated routes and roles denied.
- Give every navigation picker a fixed top Close action and existing menu links an explicit, confirmed Remove action.
- Distinguish WordPress menus from groups shown in the visitor DSA menu. A card picker can activate an existing group before adding its link, and only visible groups count as menu placement.
- Preserve the WordPress menu source, native permissions, stale-write fingerprints and public DSA projection.

## 8.0.0-rc.187 — load complete Guest editor dependencies

- Supply WordPress i18n and the native AJAX URL before the shared visual editor mounts in the private Guest document. RC186 showed the formatting toolbar but TinyMCE reported a failed wplink plugin; RC187 is the accepted visual-editor candidate. [RC187 role acceptance](docs/WORKSPACE-ROLE-ACCEPTANCE-RC187-2026-09-25.md) records the final lab tour.

## 8.0.0-rc.186 — visual Guest contribution diagnostic

- Reuse WordPress's visual editor for Guest articles, product proposals and reviewer content edits. Preserve untouched HTML on status-only saves and reject empty visual content without sending it. This stays inside the private Guest document and changes no visitor page. RC186 lab inspection found a missing WordPress editor dependency; RC187 completes the work.

## 8.0.0-rc.185 — GET media boundary without upload cost

- Let a GET of WordPress's Add Media URL load Kiwe's role guard and redirect to its private media task. Keep POST uploads, asynchronous uploads and image processing on the original lean boot path. Add a boot-lane regression contract. [Final role acceptance](docs/WORKSPACE-ROLE-ACCEPTANCE-RC187-2026-09-25.md) records the role tour.

## 8.0.0-rc.184 — direct media route diagnostic

- Added a WordPress Add Media load hook, but lab readback showed the native page still loaded. The subsequent RC185 diagnosis found that the MU loader intentionally skipped the full app for every `media-new.php` request, including GET; the extra hook was removed in RC185.

## 8.0.0-rc.183 — private media task for staff roles

- Route Editor and Author media work through a Kiwe screen that reuses WordPress's media picker and REST permissions for upload and file metadata. The Home tile and legacy media links enter this task instead of the default admin screen.
- Preserve the WordPress media authority and keep image loading off the Posts list. [Final role acceptance](docs/WORKSPACE-ROLE-ACCEPTANCE-RC187-2026-09-25.md) records the lab result.

## 8.0.0-rc.182 — shared dashboard phone geometry

- Keep one compact branded header and a single action row on phone across the private client and staff screens. Maintain the native writers and role checks beneath the shared Metro presentation.
- Place website alerts behind the Notifications bell and give an empty Design Library a direct browse action. [RC182 acceptance](docs/WORKSPACE-UI-ACCEPTANCE-RC182-2026-09-25.md) records the lab UI result.

## 8.0.0-rc.180 — single Store menu owner

- Prevent the legacy Design Context Store step from registering the native Store's `kiwe-store` slug when the Commerce app is enabled. Shop Managers can enter Store and New product without receiving the context-wide capability. Keep the legacy Store step when native Commerce is disabled.
- Skip legacy onboarding assets on the native Store document and add a two-mode menu ownership regression. [RC180 report](docs/WORKSPACE-SHOP-MENU-RC180-2026-09-25.md) records lab acceptance.

## 8.0.0-rc.179 — Shop Manager Home class resolution

- Resolve the Store Manager's SEO assistant task to its real `DSA\SEO\SEO_Refinement_Service` class. The RC178 lab role fixture uncovered a fatal in Home before the manager could reach Store; RC179 repairs it and retains the draft-aware product navigation.
- Add a release contract that checks the referenced PHP class resolves. [RC179 report](docs/WORKSPACE-PRODUCT-NAVIGATION-RC179-2026-09-25.md) records native lab acceptance.

## 8.0.0-rc.178 — product draft-aware navigation

- Let clients move between clean Store product tasks, return to products, refresh or leave the page without a false discard warning. All eight product editors report their existing draft state through one private hook; dirty edits still require confirmation.
- Keep the existing native WooCommerce writers, permissions and failed-save recovery. [RC178 report](docs/WORKSPACE-PRODUCT-NAVIGATION-RC178-2026-09-25.md) records the superseded lab diagnostic; RC179 carries the accepted correction.

## 8.0.0-rc.177 — readable Store order review

- Present native WooCommerce order totals, payment, delivery, billing and purchased items as distinct Metro cards. Label every available address/contact field and omit invalid `Product #0` references.
- Preserve the existing order completion permission, confirmation, stale-write and customer-notification behavior. [RC177 report](docs/WORKSPACE-ORDER-REVIEW-RC177-2026-09-25.md) records browser and lab acceptance.

## 8.0.0-rc.176 — role-safe product editing route

- Show the native WordPress product editor link only to the site owner. Client Administrators and Shop Managers continue to use Kiwe tasks and no longer receive a link into Kiwe's intentional 403 boundary.
- Label unsupported product details as a read-only overview, with guidance based on the actual available route. Keep the native editor guard unchanged. [RC176 report](docs/WORKSPACE-PRODUCT-ACCESS-RC176-2026-09-25.md) records acceptance separately.

## 8.0.0-rc.175 — WordPress visual product descriptions

- Use WordPress's classic visual editor in the private Store product detail task, with a Code tab for legacy shortcodes and custom layouts. The editor mounts only while a product is open; the standalone Store document loads WordPress-owned editor assets.
- Omit untouched descriptions from Kiwe product mutations. The WooCommerce writer now preserves the stored description exactly when a name, price, category or image changes, while validating and reading back edited descriptions.
- The synthetic workflow and Woo writer fixtures cover omitted content, a failed save and byte-for-byte preservation of legacy markup. [RC175 report](docs/WORKSPACE-PRODUCT-EDITOR-RC175-2026-09-25.md) tracks native lab acceptance separately.

## 8.0.0-rc.172 — Metro tiles for Guest and Notifications workflows

- Reuse the private shared Metro tile source for Guest applications and contribution availability, notification types, inbox/history, automatic post alerts and campaigns. Store overview cards inherit the same workflow primitive; native record writers, role gates and delivery rules are unchanged.
- Remove the former workflow-only card palette. Give the two Guest availability controls a compact two-column phone layout and preserve notification topic controls, campaign review and error recovery inside their existing tasks.
- [RC172 UI report](docs/WORKSPACE-METRO-TILES-RC172-2026-09-25.md) records responsive, release and disposable-lab acceptance. Ascendants remains a separate production decision.

## 8.0.0-rc.171 — shared Metro tiles on Home, Pages and Feedback

- Move the private Metro tile grammar into one shared stylesheet used by both the native Home shell and React task screens. Home shortcuts, Page records and Feedback moderation cards now follow the same color, border, shadow, type, focus and reduced-motion rules as Categories, Posts, People and Design Library.
- Keep WordPress page publishing/indexing/menu controls and native comment or WooCommerce review moderation unchanged. Compact the mobile task header when it has a single Home action. No public DSA style or Ascendants production change.
- [RC171 UI report](docs/WORKSPACE-METRO-TILES-RC171-2026-09-25.md) records release and disposable-lab acceptance.

## 8.0.0-rc.163 — site identity header and calmer client Home

- Show the site's proportional WordPress/Kiwe logo and native tagline in the private workspace headers. A role-permitted edit control opens the existing Website Identity task; staff without that capability see no edit link. When no logo exists, the site name remains the fallback.
- Consolidate the repeated story, contact, style, plan, services and conditional store Home tiles into one Website & Design Context entrance. Identity retains direct navigation to those original records and writers; other capability-derived tasks remain available.
- Project Seam's canonical spacing and radius tokens into the Home, Identity, Posts, Design Context and SEO documents without loading visitor theme overrides or a public app bundle. Browser and role checks cover wide-logo aspect ratio, mobile layout and permission gating. [Batch report](docs/WORKSPACE-BRAND-RC163-2026-09-25.md).

## 8.0.0-rc.162 — exact media SEO entry and site-branded workspace

- Add a record-specific Kiwe SEO AI route to editable Design Library files and their detail dialog. Unsaved attachment edits must be saved or discarded before leaving for AI review; media metadata and Design Context selection retain separate WordPress/Kiwe owners.
- Use the website's name/logo in the Design Library and SEO assistant headers. The assistant itself is titled **Kiwe SEO**.
- Complete local baseline, 19 responsive Design Library checks, 19 read-only native lab checks and authenticated client navigation pass. RC162 is installed on the disposable lab with private RC161 recovery; Ascendants remains separate, and Rank Math stays active until parity is proven. [Batch report](docs/MEDIA-SEO-ENTRY-RC162-2026-09-25.md).

## 8.0.0-rc.161 — editorial review and Kiwe SEO candidate

- Hide list controls while editing; add capability-scoped review, author search and native date/title sorting. Guest submissions open the Guest review task, with the Posts mutation path refusing a bypass.
- Reuse the taxonomy picker through a content-type adapter. Add exact-record Kiwe SEO AI links for saved articles and selected media. Reviewed suggestions update active Rank Math post fields while Rank Math remains installed; attachments retain WordPress media-field authority.
- Local baseline, post, SEO and responsive browser checks pass. The 426-file package and 19 read-only native checks pass on the disposable lab, with RC160 retained privately and public staging retired. Authenticated RC161 browser and actual Rank Math acceptance remain open; Ascendants activation is separate and Rank Math deactivation requires a later parity audit. [Batch report](docs/ASCENDANTS-EDITORIAL-PREDEPLOY-RC161-2026-09-25.md).

## 8.0.0-rc.160 — editorial multiword-term correction (accepted on disposable lab)

- Preserve spaces while typing new category and tag names in Posts; normalize and deduplicate the submitted names at the save boundary. The authenticated RC159 lab trial exposed this usability failure, so RC159 was not accepted for the Ascendants pilot.
- The corrected React browser check includes multiword category and tag entry. Authenticated client Administrator publishing/editing, 20 native readback and cleanup checks, the public article, served asset integrity and restored normal caching passed on the disposable lab. Rank Math is absent there; its real-site readback remains a rollout gate. Ascendants remains RC55.4 pending a separately approved production deployment. [Pilot report](docs/ASCENDANTS-EDITORIAL-PILOT-RC160-2026-09-24.md).

## 8.0.0-rc.159 — Ascendants editorial pilot candidate, superseded on lab

- Use WordPress's visual block editor in the private Posts task so existing Gutenberg articles show headings and inline images as content instead of source comments. Distinguish inline image blocks from the post's cover thumbnail.
- Put role-permitted new categories/tags above selected-first, searchable native term choices. Add a bounded private term-search route and native capability checks.
- Surface the existing Rank Math post SEO fields when Rank Math is active, otherwise Kiwe's existing SEO fields; include SEO values in stale fingerprints and read back saves. Replace the Posts/Home Kiwe header mark with the website's brand.
- Local focused PHP and synthetic browser checks passed. The disposable-lab browser trial confirmed visual Gutenberg rendering and website branding but found that new term entry removed typed spaces. RC159 was superseded before editorial acceptance. Ascendants remains RC55.4; this release is not approved for production.

## 8.0.0-rc.158 — tax classification, accepted on disposable lab

- Add private WooCommerce tax-class creation and dependency-aware deletion tiles, targeted saved-rate deletion, and native per-product tax status/class controls for the client workspace.
- Refuse Woo's class-deletion side effect on unrelated orphan tax locations; require stale-state, role, transaction and readback checks for destructive tax changes. Preserve unrelated product fields and report uncertain native saves.
- Local PHP, 78 responsive React checks and 23 self-cleaning native WooCommerce checks are recorded in [the RC158 report](docs/TAX-CLASSIFICATION-RC158-2026-09-24.md). The corrected 426-file package is active on the disposable lab with private RC157 recovery; authenticated client Store/Tax and role-boundary checks pass. Temporary public staging/jobs are retired. Ascendants remains RC55.4 awaiting a separate approved rollout.

## 8.0.0-rc.157 — configured provider and SEO continuity

- Add a credential-blind provider status card and one explicit readiness request to the existing AI workspace. The check uses the shared request/token allowance, records only sanitized outcome metadata, never retries from the UI and never runs automatically.
- Add an owner-only Rank Math migration preview and bounded 25-record worker. Supported title, description, focus phrase, robots, canonical and social fields fill only empty Kiwe fields; Rank Math source data remains untouched and authoritative while the plugin is active.
- Retain record-level pre-import state for an explicit bounded rollback. Interrupted records restore their saved state before retry, overlapping imports are refused, owner permission is rechecked in cron and opening status performs no provider request or metadata write.
- Extend Kiwe's native SEO fallback to honor migrated canonical, robots, sitemap and social metadata only when no dedicated SEO plugin is active.
- Accept RC157 on the disposable Hostinger lab with the complete local baseline, 81 focused AI checks, 31 migration checks, 17 responsive browser checks and 21 isolated native checks passing. A premature Ascendants deployment was immediately restored to exact RC55.4 package/options; its production rollout remains separate and approval-gated. [Batch report](docs/AI-SEO-PROVIDER-MIGRATION-RC157-2026-09-21.md).

## 8.0.0-rc.156 — category-aware publisher alerts

- Connect the existing `new_post` visitor choice to first-publication delivery. Kiwe snapshots the audience-table ceiling, scans at most 25 topic-index rows and addresses at most three eligible people per cron pass, while rechecking the current topic, category and channel consent before every handoff.
- Deliver registered accounts through their selected browser/PWA, email, WhatsApp or SMS routes and anonymous visitors only through an exact active PWA subscription. Publisher sites and WooCommerce sites both retain newsletter and new-post choices; commerce topics remain additive.
- Add an aggregate-only automatic-alert card and bounded status/resume control to the client Administrator's Notifications app. Contacts, user IDs, visitor hashes, message bodies, push endpoints and provider payloads never enter the client response or retained job history.
- Preserve shared-host safety: publication performs no provider call, one global worker prevents concurrent fan-out, progress is committed before external I/O, failed scheduling leaves a recoverable state, and an uncertain handoff is never replayed automatically.
- Accept RC156 on the disposable Hostinger lab with all 86 local release gates, 40 focused notification checks, 71 responsive synthetic browser checks and 29 native WordPress checks passing. Outbound transports were intercepted; fixtures, public helpers and temporary jobs were retired; only the intentional WordPress cron remains. [Batch report](docs/NOTIFICATION-PUBLISHER-ALERTS-RC156-2026-09-21.md).

## 8.0.0-rc.155 — consent-aware notification campaigns

- Add a client Administrator Campaigns task inside Notifications. The selected marketing topic and delivery channel must both be explicitly saved by each recipient; per-topic overrides, opt-outs and usable contacts determine an on-demand private audience preview.
- Queue one reviewed campaign at a time, with at most 100 recipients and three delivery attempts per cron pass. Recheck consent and channel readiness before each send, keep uncertain attempts from replaying, and show private status without message bodies or recipient data.
- Route email, WhatsApp, SMS and browser-app delivery through the existing services. An unconfigured channel, incomplete audience scan, stale preview, duplicate campaign or failed cron schedule sends nothing. The old technical form now points to the same client campaign home.
- Accept RC155 on the disposable Hostinger lab with all 86 local release gates, 70 responsive synthetic browser checks and 22 native WordPress checks passing; outbound transports were intercepted and temporary accounts, helpers and jobs were retired. [Batch report](docs/NOTIFICATION-CAMPAIGNS-RC155-2026-09-21.md).

## 8.0.0-rc.154 — notification delivery history

- Add an on-demand, two-page notification history for each permitted staff account. It reuses the bounded native inbox, reads only when opened and shows read state plus sanitized workspace, browser, email, WhatsApp and SMS outcomes.
- Record per-recipient channel acceptance, partial browser delivery, configured fallback, provider failure, missing-contact failure and unavailable-channel state without exposing contact details, endpoints or provider responses.
- Preserve role-derived topics, personal channel choices, native WordPress comment-email controls and the existing 40-item storage limit. No table, public route, polling loop or provider request is added to page load.
- Accept RC154 on the disposable Hostinger lab with all 85 local gates and 18 native WordPress checks passing; temporary accounts, audience rows, public helpers and cron jobs are retired. [Batch report](docs/NOTIFICATION-DELIVERY-HISTORY-RC154-2026-09-20.md).

## 8.0.0-rc.153 — default client app and host opcode refresh

- Make the React workspace and client shell the default Kiwe backend while retaining an explicit pre-load `false` recovery switch for either surface.
- Refresh PHP timestamps and invalidate available opcode entries after atomic activation or rollback. Shared-host workers can no longer combine old cached version constants with a newly installed integrity manifest.
- Accept the complete Guest application-to-publication journey and the default client document on the disposable lab: all 85 local gates and 19 final native WordPress checks pass, fixtures are removed, debug is off and public release artifacts are retired. [Batch report](docs/GUEST-EDITORIAL-LIFECYCLE-RC153-2026-09-20.md).

## 8.0.0-rc.152 — Guest React document bootstrap

- Open the Guest application, review and contribution area as the private React document even when WordPress assigns the native fallback menu an unexpected screen suffix. The authenticated browser check caught and closed this integration gap before the Guest batch was accepted.

## 8.0.0-rc.151 — Guest editorial lifecycle

- Complete the non-commerce Guest path from verified application through client approval, native Contributor access, immutable article submission, Editor review, native category assignment and publication status visible to the Guest.
- Deep-link private notification actions to the exact Kiwe Guest task and record. The workflow no longer depends on the default WordPress post editor.
- Restore the pending application and prior native role when role readback fails, reject hidden or terminal records from exact links, and verify category removal with two-way readback.

## 8.0.0-rc.150 — client historical SEO review

- Bring the existing bounded historical SEO job into the client SEO assistant. A client Administrator can explicitly choose a content library, see inspection and review counts, pause or resume scheduled work, and accept or dismiss suggestions within the same app document.
- Reuse the existing single-job cursor, 5-record steps, 20-proposal review buffer, shared AI allowance and WordPress-native field writers. Merely opening progress makes no provider request, and the creator retains the sitewide AI-management capability through scheduled dispatch.
- Require the exact private REST routes, nonce, same-site origin, mutation proof, current sitewide capability, per-record edit permission and proposal version. An unconfirmed native write remains pending and reports failure.
- All 85 local gates and 16 final native WordPress lab checks pass; authenticated client-browser inspection, fixture cleanup, debug-off state and public-helper retirement are verified. Configured-provider delivery/limits and supported SEO-metadata migration remain. [Batch report](docs/SEO-HISTORICAL-WORKSPACE-RC150-2026-09-20.md).

## 8.0.0-rc.149 — per-object SEO, native post tags and no-dock repair

- Add an explicit per-object SEO assistant for posts, pages, products and media. Opening the workspace spends no provider allowance; a signed-in user must request one review, inspect the proposed fields and confirm an allowlisted application to the unchanged object.
- Bind proposals to the requesting account for 20 minutes, require the original object hash at review and application, consume successful proposals once, and keep provider keys, URLs and historical batch controls out of the client document.
- Add native tag selection and bounded tag creation to Kiwe Posts. Existing-post writes verify WordPress term readback and restore the last confirmed tag set when association cannot be confirmed.
- Suppress the empty DSA dock cluster in no-dock mode while retaining the surface root, overlay launchers, PhoneKey and passkey behavior. Add focused contracts for the new SEO, post-tag and no-dock boundaries.

## 8.0.0-rc.148 — owner/client AI workspace and shared allowance

- Add a dedicated responsive React AI assistance document for the client Administrator and technical owner. Clients choose SEO and SecureTrack assistance and see aggregate request/token allowance without provider credentials or destinations.
- Reuse the original `dsa_settings.ai` policy/allowance authority and existing client opt-in options. Owner writes are allowlisted and stale-protected; client writes require the exact role capability, REST nonce, same-site origin and mutation proof.
- Keep provider keys and URLs on the protected technical setup page. Add no public route, visitor work, scheduled worker, custom table or duplicate settings authority.
- All 83 local release gates, 14 responsive browser checks and 19 final native WordPress checks pass. The 422-file package was installed atomically on the lab; settings were restored, temporary jobs/helpers retired, one intentional WordPress cron retained, and Ascendants remained untouched. [Batch report](docs/AI-WORKSPACE-RC148-2026-09-20.md).

## 8.0.0-rc.147 — grouped role, device and keyboard acceptance

20 September lab acceptance extension (no runtime change): a server WordPress cron published synthetic scheduled post/Page records at the next minute tick; native Page title, indexing and publication actions preserved block, shortcode and Bricks metadata. The guarded probe and temporary jobs were retired. One intentional lab WordPress cron job remains. [Evidence](docs/SCHEDULING-STRUCTURED-PAGES-2026-09-20.md).

- Add a native category-creation dialog with initial focus, forward/reverse tab containment, Escape handling, focus restoration and an explicit visible-focus treatment.
- Move keyboard focus into category details and post editors, then restore it to the exact originating tile when the task closes. Give the People invitation disclosure the same open/close focus continuity and explicit expanded/control relationship.
- Build React Home from each role's complete permitted task list instead of the legacy four-shortcut cap. Editors now retain Notifications and Upload media alongside Posts, Guest review and Feedback without receiving client business controls.
- Exercise Home, Identity, People, Posts, Pages, Design library, Services, Categories and DSA Menu together at desktop, tablet and phone sizes. Keep native WordPress capabilities, role redirects, route denials and cleanup as the authority for client Administrator, Editor, Author and Shop Manager acceptance.

## 8.0.0-rc.146 — Home, Design Context and DSA Menu closure

- Home now separates **Website & Design Context** from **Daily work** and links directly to identity, story, contact, brand, website plan, Services and Store setup when commerce is available. It remains a server-derived, request-free catalog.
- Identity writes only changed allowlisted fields through the existing native Design Context authority. It preserves sibling values, rejects stale or injected mutations, and supports explicit logo, dark-logo and site-icon removal while marking required setup incomplete.
- DSA Menu now selects the visitor-connected WordPress menu first, shows DSA and theme-location status without exposing technical connection controls, and supports bounded search, pagination, nesting and return-to-source-name labels.
- Exact Home and content-card navigation shortcuts now place their requested native page, category or Service first even when that record was already in the current result page, without duplicating it.
- RC145 was rejected by its new native navigation check and rolled back cleanly to RC144. RC146 carries the focused-source correction; all 83 local gates, 48 responsive browser checks and 521 native disposable-host checks pass. [Batch report](docs/HOME-DESIGN-CONTEXT-NAVIGATION-RC146-2026-09-20.md). Ascendants remains unchanged.

## 8.0.0-rc.144 — Services and service-plan closure

- Save each unconnected Design Context service plan through its own fingerprinted create, edit or remove mutation. One plan no longer rewrites neighbouring plans, and injected fields, stale state, disabled Services and invalid images remain denied.
- Keep connected Services on native WordPress posts, thumbnails, registered scalar metadata and configured taxonomies. The client editor remains text-only on overview cards and keeps media controls inside the opened service.
- Make **Manage service categories** a dedicated service-only destination. It appears only when the configured service post type has a manageable taxonomy and filters unrelated WordPress and WooCommerce groups from that screen.
- All 83 local release gates, 18 focused responsive browser checks and 513 native PHP 8.2.33 lab checks passed; fixture cleanup/settings preservation, RC143 retention, debug-off, public sample and helper/job retirement passed. [Batch report](docs/SERVICES-CLOSURE-RC144-2026-09-20.md). Ascendants remains unchanged.

## 8.0.0-rc.143 — native attachment details and explicit media curation

- Add an on-demand file-detail editor to Design Library for the native WordPress attachment title, image alt text, caption and description. The bounded library list remains lightweight and loads full metadata only after the client opens a file.
- Save one native field at a time with per-attachment capability, stale-state, input allowlist and stored-value confirmation. Non-image files cannot receive image alt metadata.
- Keep upload, metadata editing and Design Context selection independent. Editing never selects a file, and editing an already selected file preserves its explicit curation state.

## 8.0.0-rc.142 — WordPress account removal and content transfer

- Add a focused People task for client Administrators to remove an ordinary single-site account only after choosing a receiving Administrator and confirming the exact username. WordPress reassigns authored content through its native deletion API.
- Protect the current account, technical owner, identity-managed roles, multisite membership and accounts with WooCommerce orders. Deny stale, forged, cross-role and unconfirmed requests; show bounded recipients only on demand.
- All 83 release gates, 30 responsive browser checks and 502 native PHP 8.2.33 lab checks passed; fixtures/settings were preserved, RC141 retained and helpers/jobs retired. [Batch report](docs/WORDPRESS-ACCOUNT-REMOVAL-RC142-2026-09-20.md). Ascendants remains unchanged.

## 8.0.0-rc.141 — WordPress account recovery and login email

- Add focused sign-in and recovery controls to People. Client Administrators can request WordPress's password-reset email and change an editable account's native login email with repeated confirmation and readback.
- Keep reset keys and links out of the dashboard, limit reset requests per account, and protect the signed-in account, technical owner, identity-managed Kiwe User role and accounts outside the target capability boundary.
- Keep WooCommerce billing email separate from login email. All 83 release gates, 28 responsive browser checks and 494 native PHP 8.2.33 lab checks passed; fixtures/settings were preserved and helpers/jobs retired. [Batch report](docs/WORDPRESS-ACCOUNT-RECOVERY-RC141-2026-09-20.md). Ascendants remains unchanged.

## 8.0.0-rc.140 — native page publication and recovery

- Schedule, publish, hold for review, draft or keep eligible WordPress pages private from the Pages workspace using native page status and website-local/UTC dates.
- Protect the assigned home and posts-index pages from accidental unpublishing.
- List at most eight native page revisions on demand, expose only the signed-in writer's different autosave, and restore page title/content while preserving publication, hierarchy, menu order, template, image and indexing.
- Keep page cards text-only and grant the client Administrator narrow Kiwe publication/recovery authority without exposing generic page or Bricks editing. Add focused browser, transport and disposable-host native acceptance coverage. [Batch report](docs/WORDPRESS-PAGE-PUBLISHING-RECOVERY-RC140-2026-09-20.md).
- All 83 local gates, 12 focused responsive browser checks and 485 native PHP 8.2.33 lab checks passed; fixture cleanup/settings preservation and helper/job retirement passed. RC140 is on the lab, Ascendants remains recorded RC55.4 untouched. The [forecast](docs/LAUNCH-BATCH-FORECAST.md) now counts named release-sized packages.

## 8.0.0-rc.139 — withdrawn test candidate

- Disposable-host acceptance confirmed the narrow capability correction, then exposed WordPress's explicit `edit_date` requirement when scheduling a draft page. WordPress retained the draft date and normalized the requested future status, so the candidate was rejected and rolled back to RC137. RC140 marks the intentional date change through the native post API.

## 8.0.0-rc.138 — withdrawn test candidate

- Disposable-host acceptance exposed that the client Administrator's deliberate generic page-edit restriction also blocked the first page-publication implementation. The candidate was rejected and rolled back to RC137; RC139 replaces the broad dependency with explicit Kiwe publication and recovery capabilities.

## 8.0.0-rc.137 — automatic native post recovery

- Save changed title/body from an existing post editor to the signed-in writer's native WordPress autosave after five quiet seconds, without publishing or changing the visible post.
- Refuse to replace an unreviewed recovery copy; show recovery state in the editor and let the writer explicitly review, restore or discard it.
- Allow writing to resume after a restored autosave even when WordPress retains an identical hidden autosave, while keeping stale post and autosave fingerprints mandatory for every mutation.
- Keep post lists text-only and add no visitor work, shadow storage, scheduled worker or custom table. Add focused PHP, responsive browser and native WordPress acceptance coverage. [Batch report](docs/WORDPRESS-POST-AUTOMATIC-RECOVERY-RC137-2026-09-19.md).
- All 83 release gates, 23 responsive browser checks and 476 native PHP 8.2 lab checks passed; fixtures/settings were preserved and temporary helpers/jobs were retired. Ascendants remains RC55.4.

## 8.0.0-rc.136 — withdrawn test candidate

- Disposable-host acceptance exposed that the direct core autosave writer needs WordPress's post-administration functions loaded on a standalone REST request. The candidate was never accepted or deployed to Ascendants; RC137 is the forward correction.

## 8.0.0-rc.135 — private WordPress autosave recovery

- Show the signed-in writer's latest different WordPress autosave as a separate recovery copy inside the existing on-demand Saved versions task.
- Keep another writer's autosave private, require current-post and autosave fingerprints before recovery, and reuse native title/body restore while preserving publication, schedule, categories and featured image.
- Keep post cards text-only and add no list-page query; the private autosave is read only after the writer opens Saved versions.
- Add focused PHP, responsive browser and native WordPress acceptance coverage. [Batch report](docs/WORDPRESS-POST-AUTOSAVE-RECOVERY-RC135-2026-09-19.md).

## 8.0.0-rc.134 — post scheduling and saved-version recovery

- Schedule and reschedule WordPress posts in the website timezone, with future-date validation and native UTC/local date readback. Publishing a scheduled post now clears its former future date.
- Add an on-demand, bounded revision list and native title/body restore to Posts. Publication state, schedule, categories and featured image remain intact; stale post/revision proofs and role/route boundaries protect writes.
- Keep scheduled and private posts from being silently converted to drafts in the React editor. Repair lost-response reload so its post card refreshes with the editor.
- Add focused PHP and responsive browser coverage. [Batch report](docs/WORDPRESS-POST-SCHEDULING-REVISIONS-RC134-2026-09-19.md).

## 8.0.0-rc.133 — native WordPress account invitations and roles

# 8.0.0-rc.82 — bounded SecureTrack retention

Daily retention uses a zero-wait connection lock, capped deletes and an elapsed budget checked before each query. [Scope and acceptance](docs/SECURETRACK-RETENTION-RC82-2026-09-10.md).

# 8.0.0-rc.81 — release workers promptly on denied requests

Severe-request retry responses return immediately with HTTP 429, Retry-After and no-store headers; the former eight-second tarpit no longer ties up shared-host PHP workers. Existing enforcement and the optional switch remain authoritative. Includes RC80 telemetry gates and native disposable-site acceptance. [Reliability evidence](docs/TRAFFIC-RELIABILITY-RC81-2026-09-10.md).

# 8.0.0-rc.80 — optional telemetry without disabling security

Existing visitor/page-tracking controls now stop ordinary visit work before IP/session persistence and discard stale disabled timing beacons before rate-limit/database work. Public timing requires both switches; honeypots and security/PhoneKey audit paths remain available. No new settings or schema. Whole-engine shutdown is withdrawn for this task. [Scope and verification](docs/SECURETRACK-TELEMETRY-RC80-2026-09-10.md).

# 8.0.0-rc.79 — PWA endpoint page-cache exclusion

Signal WordPress and LiteSpeed explicitly that worker/manifest endpoints must bypass page caching, including disabled-worker responses. The Hostinger browser check found a stale worker despite no-store HTTP headers. Integration correction in the RC77 PWA batch.

RC79 final retirement and settings restoration passed after transient hosting failures. The [deferred traffic incident handoff](docs/ASCENDANTS-TRAFFIC-2026-09-09.md) records a withdrawn whole-engine off-switch attempt. No production disable is claimed; the founder clarified telemetry-only reduction on 10 September.

# 8.0.0-rc.78 — offline phone layout correction

Long site names and article words wrap within the offline screen. The reconnect layout respects narrow viewport sizing and includes padding within its height. Browser-discovered integration correction within the RC77 PWA batch.

# 8.0.0-rc.77 — isolated PWA caches and dependable offline reading

Live APIs never fall back to browser caches. Workers use a site-specific cache namespace, replay only current static assets and public editorial images, and retire through a valid disable response. Install no longer downloads unused islands. Offline reconnect screens provide a retry link and return 503. Password-protected articles remain excluded even after the reader unlocks them. No new capabilities or database schema. Acceptance and migration limits are recorded in docs/PWA-LIFECYCLE-RC77-2026-09-09.md.

Keep the dedicated staff Home accessible when WooCommerce would otherwise send product-only staff and Guests to My Account. Existing native object permissions and Kiwe route restrictions still apply; no new capabilities. Integration correction within the RC75 staff batch. [Evidence](docs/STAFF-WORKSPACE-RC75-2026-09-09.md).

Staff use a dedicated Kiwe Home even before the client ownership policy is enabled. Native/Woo/completed Key login destinations converge, and the legacy Dashboard route redirects only safe page requests. Staff menus keep Home and existing native task routes; Author creation works with policy off. No new capabilities or data stores. [Scope and acceptance](docs/STAFF-WORKSPACE-RC75-2026-09-09.md).

# 8.0.0-rc.74 — role-based posting entry points

Client Home exposes native New post, Posts and submission review according to capabilities. The mobile dock includes Posts alongside Website. Authors keep writing, Editors keep the existing review workflow, and Guests keep the dedicated submission path. No new permissions or post store. [Scope and acceptance](docs/POSTING-WORKSPACE-RC74-2026-09-09.md).

# 8.0.0-rc.73 — conversion-mode authentication surface

An explicit `?kiwe-auth=1` login request can load the existing DSA screen and assets while clean conversion pages remain bare. Both surface and Key feature switches still apply, as do frontend context exclusions and existing authentication checks. Login documents request private/no-store caching and no indexing. [Scope and acceptance](docs/AUTH-SURFACE-RC73-2026-09-09.md).

# 8.0.0-rc.72 — business workspace convergence

Business details now share the workspace design primitives, have direct section navigation and honest unsaved/submitting states around the existing native POST. Text-only sections skip media-picker assets. Story and Brand point to the existing Teams and Design library destinations; clients see service connection status while the owner retains its original binding control. Fix desktop Home column specificity. No new data store, visitor script, endpoint or capability. [Scope and acceptance](docs/BUSINESS-WORKSPACE-RC72-2026-09-09.md).

# 8.0.0-rc.71 — client Home native widget registration

The actual WordPress dashboard skipped Kiwe Home because its widget title was empty. Register a translated title so the native renderer invokes the Home callback. Add native meta-box rendering coverage and grouped RC65–RC68 lab acceptance; no data, permission or visitor-runtime change. [Lab acceptance and build comparison](docs/WORKSPACE-LAB-RC71-2026-09-09.md).

# 8.0.0-rc.70 — native schema acceptance and RC55 backport

Native PHP 8.2/MariaDB acceptance found that dbDelta deliberately DESCRIBEs missing tables. The coordinator now stops failed writes while permitting these metadata probes. Initial creation, zero-DDL repeats and independent-connection locking are verified. Production backport 8.0.0-rc.55.2 contains only the four SecureTrack files plus version/manifest changes; pending workspace features stay local. See docs/SECURETRACK-DEPLOYMENT-2026-09-08.md for exact deployment state.

# Changelog


## 8.0.0-rc.69 — SecureTrack schema repair, 8 September 2026

- Removed automatic schema repair from request/bootstrap and event/session failure paths. Explicit native POST or opted-in CLI maintenance now owns schema changes, with a connection lock, ownership checks, cooldown and verified completion.
- Corrected dbDelta declaration spacing and integer widths that caused repeated ALTER statements on matching tables. Reused equivalent indexes and corrected schema readiness caching.
- [Incident evidence, compatibility and acceptance](docs/SECURETRACK-SCHEMA-RC69-2026-09-08.md). Local only; the restored Ascendants RC55 installation still needs a separately verified deployment.


## 8.0.0-rc.68 — Categories and Team, 8 September 2026

- Converged Categories and Team on shared app tokens, responsive cards and native task controls. Added optional unsaved/undo/submitting feedback and post-selection counts around existing WordPress POST saves.
- Added stale-form comparison and confirmed native readback to category renames and team edits. Preserved native fields, capabilities and explicit partial-move outcomes.
- [Scope, compatibility and local acceptance](docs/WORKSPACE-ORGANIZATION-RC68-2026-09-08.md). No new settings store, endpoint or visitor bundle; local only, lab stays RC64.

## 8.0.0-rc.67 — setup reminders, 8 September 2026

- Replaced the long account-enrollment paragraph with a concise security warning, existing sign-in action and native expandable remaining steps. Security state and enrollment rules are unchanged.
- Made the business reminder a Home task for actors allowed to edit Design Context, linking to the existing Identity controls. Completion still comes from native onboarding state.
- Added shared, scoped notice styling with no JavaScript or new persistence, plus focused current-actor, completion, disabled and rendering regressions.
- [Scope, verification and pending native acceptance](docs/WORKSPACE-NOTICES-RC67-2026-09-08.md). Local only; lab remains RC64.

## 8.0.0-rc.66 — reactive tasks, 8 September 2026

- Added optional same-origin menu-label and Design library selection saves around the existing native writers, with exact role/object checks and private response projections.
- Preserved other pending edits, confirmed menu fingerprints through readback, checked stale selections and blocked related writes after an unconfirmed response. Native forms remain available without JavaScript.
- Extended shared app cards and responsive controls to both task screens without a public bundle, new persistence or client framework. Added executable server/script regressions and a prepared native fixture.
- [Scope, local verification and acceptance limits](docs/WORKSPACE-TASKS-RC66-2026-09-08.md). Local only; lab stays RC64 and Ascendants RC55.

- Added a small client-workspace projection of the existing DSA/Seam token registry, role-aware Home cards and native mobile navigation. Technical owner and disabled ownership policy retain the native dashboard.
- Added progressive page-name saves through one cookie-authenticated WordPress REST adapter around the existing native writer, with stale-edit/readback checks and a native POST fallback. The UI preserves unsaved text, blocks duplicate submission, bounds network waiting and confirms saved state only from the server.
- Corrected native Home menu renaming and restricted publishing counters to roles allowed to inspect other authors. No new settings store, front-end bundle or AI call.
- Added grouped executable Home/REST/browser-script checks and a focused native lab fixture. Scope, evidence and remaining convergence work: [RC65 workspace record](docs/WORKSPACE-APP-RC65-2026-09-08.md).

- Tightened the local form test double to the real helper signature and added native whole-screen rendering proof. No new SEO features.
- RC59–RC63 native acceptance: 37 checks and an eight-process allowance/lease race passed on the disposable lab. RC64 focused native rendering and responsive/browser submission acceptance passed; temporary artifacts and jobs removed, settings preserved, Ascendants unchanged. See [the grouped lab record](docs/RC64-LAB-ACCEPTANCE-2026-09-07.md).

## 8.0.0-rc.63 — local, 6 September 2026

- Replaced full-catalog score reads with 25-record projections, bounded counters, dirty generations and a leased read-only worker. Completed reports publish atomically; stale reports remain visible during refresh.
- Native content/business changes enqueue work. Full onboarding saves no longer calculate the entire score. The technical SEO home adds a progress/update control independent of AI.
- Excluded protected/private content and unavailable Woo products; empty phrase arrays and old AI approval no longer earn readiness points. Retained legacy snapshot fallback and documented downgrade semantics.

## 8.0.0-rc.62 — local, 6 September 2026

- Replaced growing SEO ID/proposal job state with paged discovery, a twenty-proposal review buffer, CAS state updates and fenced 120-second leases. Added pause/resume/cancel and quota-aware manual recovery.
- Reject stale reviews, recheck public visibility and permissions after AI, and verify native writes before recording acceptance. Long-running broker checks refresh owner/client policy caches.
- Reused scoped task cards and stored score snapshots; kept legacy jobs inert/preserved and documented rollback. Added deterministic job boundary regressions and a focused reserve recipe.

## 8.0.0-rc.61 — local token allowance build

Reserves shared request and estimated token allowance together, then settles supported Gemini usage through bounded idempotent receipts. Failed/unknown responses retain estimates; usage above reservation is charged and pauses AI. Adds native owner token ceilings and read-only client usage. Preserves existing request/cooldown state during schema-2 migration; older runtimes pause inference on rollback. [Scope and acceptance](docs/AI-TOKEN-ALLOWANCE-RC61-2026-09-06.md). Local only; lab remains RC58.

Separates SEO owner permission from SiteGraph enrichment. Adds one client AI assistance workspace with independent SEO/SecureTrack choices and aggregate request usage. The broker requires both owner permission and client opt-in; upgrade defaults remain off. Existing provider settings, deterministic SEO and security remain authoritative. [Scope, compatibility and acceptance](docs/AI-FEATURE-PERMISSIONS-RC60-2026-09-06.md). Local only; lab remains RC58.

## 8.0.0-rc.59 — local AI allowance build

Adds shared site request ceilings, atomic reservations, conservative failed-attempt accounting, direct-provider 429 cooldowns and existing AI-page controls. SEO displays next eligible attempt times. Fixes Gemini Interactions omitting the configured output-token limit. Request counts are not a monetary cap. [Scope and acceptance](docs/AI-ALLOWANCE-RC59-2026-09-06.md); not deployed or signed.

- Add a responsive Design library with explicit native attachment selection across all WordPress file types, plus selection of approved public comments/Woo reviews as testimonial evidence. Bound queries and recheck public visibility; no automatic whole-library enrolment.
- Narrow Design Context media exports to selected records; expose a bounded, private-field-free testimonial projection with source links and excerpt disclosure. Existing identity/team/product/service image assignments retain their purpose.

- Add shared scoped task cards and Home/page/category shortcuts. Lab remains RC56; see docs/DESIGN-LIBRARY-NAVIGATION-2026-09-06.md for grouped verification and compatibility requirements.

## 8.0.0-rc.57 — 2026-09-06 (local candidate)

- Add bounded page-name editing to Page details, preserving the existing URL and layout. Dedicated capability, page-bound nonce, stale-form detection and actor-bound success receipts retain the client/technical-owner boundary.
- Route Guest product proposals to opted-in store reviewers through a dedicated topic and native review link. Require a matching pending Guest submission; article review remains separate.

## 8.0.0-rc.56 — 2026-09-06 (candidate)

- Validate the full origin (scheme, host and effective port) against configured WordPress home/site URLs. Explicit opaque or malformed Origin/Referer values and Host-derived foreign origins are rejected; configured frontend/admin origins, subdirectories, development ports, IPv6 and legacy absent-header requests remain supported. Existing mutation proof and downstream nonce/capability checks remain required. Adds 33 behavioral checks covering the production guard, hydration permission callback and mutation guard to the green baseline.
- Capture the founder's product vision, current engineering roadmap and maintained contribution/handoff contract. Add repository-local contributor instructions and PR templates, resolve conflicting branch guidance, and identify historical execution documents clearly.
- Add native Categories and Teams task screens: category name/create and bounded selected-post moves; eligible existing team members, public titles and display order. Server-side capability/object/nonce checks remain authoritative, with partial failures reported and page-scoped CSS only.
- Link routine Administrator tasks from Workspace Home; remove unsupported health/protection claims and label channel configuration/test evidence accurately.
- Maintain founder intent, task boundaries, reserve-session recipes and source-hashed evidence tooling. Correct Hostinger install order, canonical paths and the non-PHP emergency-disable filename.
- Add a verified unsigned folder-copy ZIP builder after the Hostinger lab rejected the Windows tar archive's consistency. Capture provider-aware backups, portable snapshots and media archive/offload boundaries as a researched proposal, without enabling a backup product or Drive integration.
- See `docs/WORKSPACE-ENGINEERING-2026-09-06.md` for exact verification/deployment state and `docs/ENGINEERING-BASELINE-2026-09-05.md` for the earlier lead-transition evidence. RC56 is a distinct candidate; published RC55 artifacts remain immutable. Signed-feed publication and production rollout are separate, unperformed operations.

## 8.0.0-rc.55 — 2026-09-05

- Keep WordPress as the sole password authority while presenting its isolated login, lost-password and reset-password document as a responsive, site-branded Key.kiwe surface. The Design Context logo replaces WordPress branding, and reset bearer keys remain off normal theme pages, advertisements and analytics.
- Correct new-device recovery to assert an existing or synced passkey first. A new credential is offered only when that device genuinely needs one, preventing WebAuthn's duplicate-credential rejection after a user already enrolled a passkey on the same relying party.
- Turn duplicate WebAuthn registration into a clear route back to existing-passkey assertion, and detect in-app browsers without passkey APIs before opening an unusable authenticator prompt. The recovery screen explains that moving into a full browser creates a separate secure browser context and therefore repeats verification.
- Make OTP destination language explicit: email identifiers use email, phone identifiers use the configured WhatsApp/SMS lane, and the configured email fallback is labelled as such instead of being presented as phone delivery.
- Extend strict-flow release gates for site-owned password branding, assertion-first device recovery, duplicate-credential handling and in-app browser guidance.

## 8.0.0-rc.54 — 2026-09-05

- Complete the Key.kiwe-to-WordPress privilege handoff without ever emailing or exposing a plaintext password. Elevating an account into any administrator-area role now revokes prior assurance, queues WordPress' native one-time set/reset-password link, and greets the user with the exact first-access instructions inside Key.kiwe.
- Repair already-promoted Key-created accounts on their next identification attempt. Password creation, native password reset and successful password proof now advance one explicit setup state while still invalidating old sessions, trusted devices and privileged assurance before strict recovery-contact and passkey verification.
- Make OTP journeys feel immediate even when SMTP or WhatsApp is slow. Key.kiwe renders the destination-aware code screen first, delivers through the existing bounded resend endpoint in a background request, and keeps verification/resend controls disabled until the provider accepts the message.
- Make approved Guest identity unambiguous with one read-only `✓ Guest` badge and no concurrent application action.
- Make Menu table-of-contents selection replace Kiwe's synthetic Sheet history entry with the real heading anchor, preventing a delayed browser history restoration from undoing the reader's jump while retaining sticky-header clearance.
- Extend release gates for privileged password enrollment, deferred OTP delivery, approved Guest presentation and TOC/history continuity.

## 8.0.0-rc.53 — 2026-09-04

- Introduce the first production Kiwe Workspace shell for every approved wp-admin role except the single Super Admin. It restyles native WordPress administration instead of duplicating posts, media, pages, categories, users or Design Context data.
- Replace the client dashboard redirect with a server-rendered, role-aware Home: Administrator, Editor, Author, Guest and Shop Manager receive only safe actions already authorized by Kiwe's capability and route boundary.
- Keep the dashboard honest and light. Publishing and category cards read canonical WordPress data; Email and Key.kiwe status pills require actual delivery/provider evidence; editorial sites do not render WooCommerce actions.
- Load one CSS file only for signed-in non-owner wp-admin requests. No front-end asset, REST hydration, animation, glass effect or new JavaScript is added, and responsive tablet/phone layouts reuse WordPress' native mobile navigation.
- Add release contracts covering owner isolation, dashboard access, canonical data, role-aware actions, channel evidence, responsive behavior and the zero-JavaScript shell.

## 8.0.0-rc.52 — 2026-09-03

- Establish one Kiwe-wide notification ingress for SecureTrack, Key.kiwe, editorial, Guest, WooCommerce, PWA and future modules. Sources publish a protected topic and event; the shared router owns the inbox, browser/app push and the Email, WhatsApp and SMS gateways.
- Replace SecureTrack's private `wp_mail()` lane with the shared notification pipeline. SecureTrack retains site-wide severity, repeat-suppression and hourly-budget authority, while each authorized administrator selects the SecureTrack topic and any configured delivery channels in WordPress → Notifications.
- Keep security evidence independent from notification delivery: every observation remains in SecureTrack even when a recipient disables a topic/channel or a gateway is unavailable. Simplified client Administrators can also read and acknowledge the shared inbox through their dedicated notification-policy capability.
- Document the stable extension contract so future notification types register one authorized topic and publish through `kiwe_notification_event` without owning credentials or duplicating channel logic.

## 8.0.0-rc.51 — 2026-09-03

- Make Guest contributions explicitly opt-in under WordPress → Guest. Guest posts and WooCommerce-only product proposals have independent site-owner controls; both are disabled until deliberately enabled, and product controls remain dormant when WooCommerce is unavailable.
- Restrict the Profile DSA application control to ordinary accounts that have verified at least one Key.kiwe factor and have no administrator-area role or capability. Pending, denied and approved Guest status remains visible without reopening the application action.
- Enforce the same feature flags at the REST application boundary and submission boundary, so hidden controls, forged requests and previously approved accounts cannot submit a contribution type the site owner has paused.
- Keep client Administrators subscribed to owner-relevant order and visitor topics through Kiwe's dedicated notification-policy capability even though their simplified workspace deliberately removes WordPress `manage_options`; Shop Managers remain product-only and do not inherit order alerts.

## 8.0.0-rc.50 — 2026-09-03

- Turn the built-in Contributor compatibility role into an application-gated Guest relationship. A partially verified Key.kiwe account can apply from Profile DSA for guest articles and, only when WooCommerce is active, product proposals; Administrator/Super Admin approval is required and the Profile badge reflects pending, denied or Guest state.
- Add an isolated create-once Guest workspace. Guests can submit read-only article drafts or product proposals and see only their own contribution status; native Posts, Media, REST mutation, builder and unrelated wp-admin capabilities remain unavailable. Product proposals never grant order, payout or store-settings access.
- Move role-relevant notification preferences into a first-class WordPress Notifications menu backed by Kiwe's existing preference and delivery services. Unconfigured email, WhatsApp, SMS and app lanes are disabled, and Guest/application/editorial events reuse the same inbox, push and channel dispatchers.
- Surface SecureTrack's existing severity, recipient and flood-control policy in the Notifications center for client Administrators and the Super Admin without creating a second security-alert store. Add executable application, role-injection, REST-bypass, gateway-availability and Profile DSA contracts.

## 8.0.0-rc.49 — 2026-09-03

- Lock Bricks builder and technical settings to the Super Admin under the active ownership policy, including Bricks' cached raw grants. Show effective builder access in Users.
- Use native WordPress Pages for clients: view pages, create titled draft pages and change indexing; no content/layout editing or deletion of existing pages.
- Remove the duplicate page inventory/editor and planned-page repeater from Website plan. Preserve legacy notes; infer current pages from WordPress.
- Synchronize index/noindex with Rank Math's native robots metadata, preserving unrelated directives and the native sitemap/context mirror. Add executable draft/nonce/forged-input/indexing tests and optional Bricks vendor-source proof.

## 8.0.0-rc.48 — 2026-09-03

- Added an explicitly activated single-owner client workspace. The full-access Super Admin is protected from deletion, demotion, competing assignments and client credential edits. Ownership transfer requires the current owner's password, uses an atomic lock and restores previous assignments on persistence failure; native Multisite ownership remains a singleton.
- Added capability, direct-admin-route and API enforcement for simplified client Administrators, read-only Pages, editorial roles and product-only Shop Managers. Rank Math offers owner-configured inline/dashboard/off access, with post-bound SEO writes. Frontend Key, saved items, native media, Bricks forms/query flows and telemetry retain their existing authorization.
- Added the verified User lifecycle without replacing staff roles. Extra role retirement records previous definitions and user assignments; no accounts/posts are deleted by migration.
- Moved Identity, Story, Contact, Brand, Website plan, Services and conditional Store into standalone WordPress menus. Removed Review, retained secure legacy handoff links, scoped form saves to their own native data and removed synchronous site-wide SEO scans from those saves.
- Added executable ownership/rollback, capability/API isolation, independent form saves and standalone-section JavaScript tests. Live deployment and role-session evidence are recorded separately from local test results.

## 8.0.0-rc.47 — 2026-09-03

- Made Design Context's Store step conditional on an active commerce integration, separated Services into an explicit opt-in section, and removed the duplicate Resources picker in favor of the existing bounded, paginated Media Library catalog (all MIME types).
- Replaced the parallel team editor with administrator-controlled WordPress User membership/title fields. Public team context and existing Bricks/WordPress bindings now resolve selected eligible staff directly from user records, preserving old stable selectors. Legacy unlinked notes are retained but no longer published.
- Added staff public-profile self-editing to the existing DSA profile form: biography, website, LinkedIn and Facebook. Selection/title remain administrator-only; account email, phone, passwords, roles, sessions and arbitrary plugin metadata are not part of public team exports.
- Native identity, page visibility and WooCommerce settings now override stale saved Design Context snapshots. Disabled service plans are retained without service-post writes. Public media queries exclude private, draft and password-protected parent records.
- Added executable user-authority, public-data, service opt-in and commerce/non-commerce wizard-navigation regression tests.

## 8.0.0-rc.46 — 2026-09-02

- Made every newly opened DSA panel start at its own top without disturbing the reader's preserved page position, and stopped the active table-of-contents tracker from forcing the Menu sheet down to a centered item.
- Added lightweight current-post category context to the existing Menu payload, so a category navigation item is highlighted while reading one of its posts without adding an extra query on non-post routes.
- Added `aria-current="page"` to the active category or route link while retaining `aria-current="location"` for the active table-of-contents heading.

## 8.0.0-rc.45 — 2026-09-02

- Rebranded the public authentication product from PhoneKey to Key.kiwe across Kiwe administrator and user-facing copy, and changed the default gateway to `https://key.kiwelaunch.com/v1/otp`.
- Added a compatibility migration that upgrades the exact former gateway URL automatically while retaining invisible legacy identifiers only where removing them would break existing WordPress identity, settings, sessions, or encrypted transport continuity.
- Migrated signed gateway requests to `X-Kiwe-Key-*` headers while keeping temporary server-side aliases during the controlled multi-site rollout.
- Paired Kiwe with the new Key.kiwe multi-tenant account model: one owner primary WhatsApp connection is inherited by default, while each website can switch to, replace, remove, or restore a dedicated client connection.

## 8.0.0-rc.44 — 2026-09-02

- Clarified the account lifecycle without inventing a WordPress role: an ordinary PhoneKey signup is a low-friction Subscriber, one verified email or phone makes that account purchase-ready, and only a completed WooCommerce order transitions a Subscriber-only account to Customer.
- Closed direct checkout entry: when the Kiwe identity gate or WooCommerce's own account requirement is active, typing the checkout URL now creates a same-origin checkout intent, opens PhoneKey/Profile, and exposes the Checkout DSA only after the identity boundary is satisfied.
- Closed the Checkout REST contract and draft lane until identity is allowed, preserving classic checkout and Checkout Block server enforcement as the final boundary.
- Deferred every WooCommerce purchase hook until WooCommerce has actually loaded, so editorial/non-commerce sites attach no purchase-gate, order-transition, or checkout-entry hooks.
- Preserved Bricks' native checkout-template authority after reviewing its WooCommerce registration-required path; Kiwe guards entry before Bricks renders and returns to the configured DSA/native checkout journey afterward.

## 8.0.0-rc.43 — 2026-09-02

- Made Subscriber the default PhoneKey signup role on editorial and WooCommerce sites, with a one-time migration from the former automatic Customer default while preserving the administrator's ability to change the signup role afterward.
- Added one-step first signup for an ordinary Subscriber: a new email or phone identity can enter immediately for bookmarks and wishlists, while an existing account still requires proof so identifier knowledge alone can never take over a user.
- Turned bookmark and wishlist clicks into authenticated intents. Logged-out visitors enter the existing PhoneKey Profile sheet, the same-origin intent survives the login redirect in session storage, and the item is written to canonical WordPress user meta only after authentication.
- Added a WooCommerce purchase identity gate: checkout requires a signed-in WordPress account and either a verified email or verified phone, while full email + phone + passkey completion remains a stronger optional Profile state for ordinary customers.
- Enforced the purchase gate in both classic WooCommerce checkout and Checkout Block/Store API order placement, keeping the Profile sheet as guidance rather than the only security boundary.
- Made WooCommerce Customer a purchase lifecycle role: only a Subscriber-only account transitions to Customer after its first classic or Checkout Block order, so purchases never overwrite editorial, store-management, administrator, or custom multi-role access.
- Separated the built-in post type's create capability from edit/publish capability: Editor can update and publish existing stories but cannot create new posts; Author retains native creation and ownership boundaries.
- Restricted Author and Editor wp-admin navigation to the post, media, and own-profile lane, and made every role with editorial admin access enter strict PhoneKey password, recovery, phone, and passkey enrollment.
- Restricted Shop Manager to WooCommerce product viewing, creation, editing, publishing, existing taxonomy assignment, and product-media support routes; deletion, taxonomy management, and every unrelated wp-admin capability, menu, and route are removed while Administrator and Multisite Super Admin remain deliberately unchanged for the later policy batch.
- Added Phone and PhoneKey verification columns to the native WordPress Users list, so contact and security state remain visible where WordPress roles are managed.
- Added release contracts for subscriber defaults, one-step signup safety, saved-intent replay, classic/block checkout enforcement, editorial capability boundaries, strict admin enrollment, and native Users integration.

## 8.0.0-rc.42 — 2026-09-02

- Preserved the reader's exact document position through Sheet close and Kiwe's synthetic history release, while keeping deliberate table-of-contents jumps exempt from the restoration step.
- Made Menu identify the current H1/H2/H3 immediately on open and bring its active table-of-contents entry into view, so opening navigation mid-article exposes the reader's current section.
- Added a real Kiwe > Menu navigation-size control with Compact, Balanced, and Expressive scales; Compact is the lightweight editorial default and is smaller than RC41's fixed scale.
- Restored native AdSense vignette authority for Search and other DSA result links by no longer removing or intercepting the clicked result, with an exact same-origin destination fail-safe after the ad closes.
- Added Latest and Popular sorting pills to Search. Popular reuses SecureTrack's existing page-view table through a decoupled filter, runs only on explicit Popular requests, compares a bounded candidate set, and remains covered by the five-minute Search cache.
- Added release contracts for reading-progress restoration, visible current-section state, configurable Menu scale, AdSense destination continuity, deterministic sort requests, and SecureTrack-backed popularity.

## 8.0.0-rc.41 — 2026-09-02

- Made new-device and ordinary OTP screens identify the server-selected destination with a privacy-safe email or phone hint, so users know where to retrieve the code without exposing the full contact unnecessarily.
- Removed inactive trust claims from PhoneKey and restricted the payment-protection badge to sites with an active commerce capability, keeping editorial publishers free of misleading ecommerce language.
- Reduced the legacy Menu navigation scale for long category lists while preserving Kiwe's responsive type tokens and administrator-authored navigation copy.
- Added lightweight, animation-frame-coalesced table-of-contents tracking that marks the in-view H1/H2/H3 entry with the configured active color and releases its listeners when the Menu sheet is gone.
- Made TOC navigation measure WordPress and theme sticky headers before scrolling, keeping the selected article heading visible instead of hiding it underneath the publisher header.
